- **spec**: When a local-only command receives an `ssh://` repository locator as the repository it acts on, it SHALL reject with `kind: "unsupportedOnRemote"`, naming the command, without contacting the server. This SHALL NOT apply to local-only commands that reach a locator's host themselves, such as connecting to a host, testing a connection, or opening a remote repository's terminal. ([Siomkin/GitLane](https://github.com/Siomkin/GitLane/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-10-30-remote-server-terminal/specs/ipc/commands/spec.md) · ★7)

Provider tokens, HTTPS passwords, URL-embedded credentials, and one-time device codes MUST be replaced by `***`, whether or not the text contains a URL. This MUST cover recognised bare tokens, `Authorization` header values, and token query parameters, and the sam ([Siomkin/GitLane](https://github.com/Siomkin/GitLane/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-11-08-enforce-critical-invariants/specs/ipc/commands/spec.md) · ★7)

- **spec**: A command argument that names one of a fixed set of choices SHALL be rejected at the command boundary when it holds a value outside that set. This covers merge methods, PR state actions, reset modes, and in-progress operation kinds. No provider or write may substitute a default choice for an unknown value. ([Siomkin/GitLane](https://github.com/Siomkin/GitLane/blob/HEAD/openspec/changes/archive/2026-10-09-type-the-ipc-and-write-boundary/specs/ipc/commands/spec.md) · ★7)
- **spec**: A write command that takes more than six inputs beyond the repository locator MUST accept them as a single request object with named fields rather than as positional arguments. - **spec-driven-development**: 仕様駆動開発プロセスを起動するスキル。要件→設計→実装計画→実装の4フェーズを、 エージェント協調と3段階エスカレーション（Phase 1/2/3）で進行させる。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合は必ずこのスキルを使用する。 - `/spec-driven-development [タスク]` の明示的呼び出しがあった - 新規機能・システムを仕様策定から実装完了まで体系的に進めたい - 「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの仕様精度を全フェーズで確保したい - 複数の専門領域（基本設計・セキュリティ・アーキテクチャ・TDD・Git・ コードレビュー）を協調させて実装を進めたい - エージェント間の判断対立や判断不能を体系的にエスカレーションしたい - 仕様の決定背景・代替案・トレードオフを文書として残したい - 実装中の曖昧性を排除し、再現可能な開発プロセスで進行させたい 各フェーズには時間制約（§0.5）、リスク分類基準（§0.3）、対立解消手順（§5）が 定義されており、エスカレーション発生時は §0.6 ログスキーマに記録される。 単発の実装タスクや既存コードの軽微な修正には使用しない。 ([YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData](https://github.com/YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/spec-driven-development.md))

- **specai-mini**: Run SpecAI in compact mode for a small feature or bug fix. ([ArceApps-dev/specai](https://github.com/ArceApps-dev/specai/blob/HEAD/commands/specai-mini.md))
- **specai-plan**: Run SpecAI’s canonical workflow from its plan skill. ([ArceApps-dev/specai](https://github.com/ArceApps-dev/specai/blob/HEAD/commands/specai-plan.md))
- **specai-review**: Review current diff for over-engineering (delete/stdlib/native/yagni/shrink tags). ([ArceApps-dev/specai](https://github.com/ArceApps-dev/specai/blob/HEAD/commands/specai-review.md))
- **specai-verify**: Verify implementation against acceptance criteria. ([ArceApps-dev/specai](https://github.com/ArceApps-dev/specai/blob/HEAD/commands/specai-verify.md))
- **specai-write-propose**: Create the five technical feature documents from an approved PRD, then present one proposal decision. ([ArceApps-dev/specai](https://github.com/ArceApps-dev/specai/blob/HEAD/commands/specai-write-propose.md))
- **specflow**: Write or continue project documents with the specflow skill ([vuquoctrungbk/specflow](https://github.com/vuquoctrungbk/specflow/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/commands/specflow.md))
- **specforge-analyze**: Audit the active feature's artifacts against each other and report gaps. Read-only — it produces findings, it does not change code. Delegate to the `reviewer` subagent for an independent pass. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-analyze.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-approve**: Record a human's approval of a stage. Approvals are deliberate human acts — this skill is **user-invoked only** and never auto-triggers. Approving **both** `specify` and `plan` for the active feature is what unblocks code edits (the PreToolUse gate). ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-approve.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-clarify**: Convert the spec's `[NEEDS CLARIFICATION]` markers into concrete decisions using a **questions-in-a-file** flow (from AI-DLC): more reviewable and diffable than chatting, and it keeps a record. Run this between `/specforge-specify` and `/specforge-plan`. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-clarify.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-constitution**: Define the small set of engineering principles this project commits to. `/specforge-plan` checks every plan against them and must document compliance or an explicit exception. These are **soft gates** (advisory) — the hard, blocking gate is spec + plan approval. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-constitution.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-implement**: Execute `tasks.md` top-to-bottom to implement the active feature. This is deliberately **user-invoked only** (not auto-triggered) so building is an explicit decision. The PreToolUse gate will refuse code edits unless the feature's `specify` **and** `plan` are approved — so if you haven't approved yet, do that first. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-implement.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-init**: Create the committed `specforge/` workspace that every other SpecForge command reads and writes. Run this once per project. It is safe to re-run: never overwrite an existing file — only create what is missing and report what already existed. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-init.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-loop**: Drive the active feature's `tasks.md` to completion by executing tasks one at a time in a loop, checking each off as you go. This is SpecForge's take on the **Ralph loop** (run the agent repeatedly until the work is done) — but made **safe**: it can only ever execute a plan a human already approved, and it stops instead of guessing when it hits a blocker. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-loop.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-operate**: Close the lifecycle. After a feature is built, turn it into something that can be **shipped and owned**: a concrete deploy plan, a runbook, observability, a rollback plan, and a handoff. This is AI-DLC's **Operations** phase. It *plans* operations — it does **not** execute deployments. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-operate.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-plan**: Translate an approved spec into a concrete technical plan. This is the **Construction** design step. Consider delegating to the `architect` subagent. The human's approval of this plan is what **unblocks code edits** (together with the spec approval). ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-plan.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-quick**: The escape hatch that keeps SpecForge from becoming ceremony for a one-line fix. It flips `fasttrack: on` so the gate allows code edits without a full spec+plan — and records that choice in the audit log. **User-invoked only.** ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-quick.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-resume**: Reconstruct context from the on-disk state (which survives context compaction) and continue the lifecycle at the first unfinished step. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-resume.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-reverse-engineer**: Most real adoption is on existing code, not greenfield. This skill analyzes the current repo and produces a baseline set of artifacts so the team can move forward under the lifecycle. Delegate the heavy analysis to the `reverse-engineer` subagent to keep the main context clean. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-reverse-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-specify**: Turn a feature request into a testable specification. This is the **Inception** phase: describe the problem, users, and requirements — *not* the implementation. Delegate the drafting to the `spec-analyst` subagent when the feature is non-trivial. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-specify.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-status**: Render a clear, human-readable snapshot from `specforge/state.md` and the feature dirs. Read-only. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-status.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-tasks**: Derive small, ordered, individually verifiable tasks from `plan.md` and `spec.md`. Each task traces to the requirement(s) it satisfies so nothing is missed. - **speckit.ac.critic**: Independent critic review of a feature's spec, plan, tasks, acceptance criteria (ac) or feature files (features): an Opus sub-agent writes each review to critic-&lt;stage&gt;-pass-&lt;n&gt;.md in the feature folder; spec, plan, tasks and ac iterate verify → revise → re-review up to 3 passes. Started only by the user. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/ac/commands/speckit.ac.critic.md))
- **speckit.ac.init**: AC setup, once per project: detect the .NET solution and test setup, ask what can't be detected, write .workflow/config.json, create the Reqnroll acceptance-test project and verify the setup; `smoke` runs the live guard smoke test. Started only by the user. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/ac/commands/speckit.ac.init.md))
- **speckit.ac.tests-analyze**: AC step 6: run every feature's acceptance tests against the real implementation and, if any fail, have the isolated ac-test-fail-judge decide whether the implementation, the test, the translation or an outdated acceptance criterion is at fault. Started only by the user. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/ac/commands/speckit.ac.tests-analyze.md))
- **speckit.ac.tests-write**: AC step 5: export the public API as reference assemblies and have the isolated ac-test-writer write the Reqnroll step definitions for a feature, without seeing the implementation. Started only by the user. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/ac/commands/speckit.ac.tests-write.md))
- **speckit.ac.translate**: AC step 2–3: draft a feature's acceptance.md for review and lock it, then have the isolated ac-feature-translator turn it into Reqnroll .feature files. Started only by the user. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/ac/commands/speckit.ac.translate.md))
- **speckit.adr.audit**: Audit the codebase against accepted ADRs. For each ADR, surface code patterns that contradict the decision and code patterns the ADR mandates but cannot be found. Severity is derived from the ADR's `criticality` field. Findings respect the central waiver registry at `.specify/waivers.yml`. ([Satcomx00-x00/SoloW](https://github.com/Satcomx00-x00/SoloW/blob/HEAD/.specify/presets/nextjs/commands/speckit.adr.audit.md))
- **speckit.adr.new**: Scaffold a new Architecture Decision Record using MADR 4 (full) format at docs/adr/. Auto-numbers, links to the current commit, prompts for status, drivers, options, and consequences. The ADR becomes part of the project's durable memory — read by /speckit.context.refresh and enforced by /speckit.adr.audit. ([Satcomx00-x00/SoloW](https://github.com/Satcomx00-x00/SoloW/blob/HEAD/.specify/presets/nextjs/commands/speckit.adr.new.md))
- **speckit.adr.supersede**: Mark an existing ADR as superseded and scaffold its replacement. Preserves the audit trail — the old ADR keeps its content, gains a `superseded_by` link, and the index updates its status. The new ADR carries a back-reference and inherits the old ADR's constitution_refs unless overridden. ([Satcomx00-x00/SoloW](https://github.com/Satcomx00-x00/SoloW/blob/HEAD/.specify/presets/nextjs/commands/speckit.adr.supersede.md))
